# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 395–424

## Page 431 — IIIF Canvas #395

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0395.JP2` (5811 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0395.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0395.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 431 (recto) features three medical-alchemical recipes in the left column (a gold-based resolvent for fevers, a sudorific extract, and a mercury precipitate) and an illustrated treatise on boiling good soap in three or four hours in the right column.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Soap-boiler at a furnace with a wooden lye tub** (`#xywh=0,0,5811,6853`): Framed watercolor and pen-and-ink illustration at the top of the right column depicting a man in a green tunic, black cap, and red breeches standing beside a brick hearth with a fire burning under a round cauldron, sprinkling powder while stirring with a rod; to the right stands a large hooped wooden tub on three legs.

## Page 432 — IIIF Canvas #396

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0396.JP2` (6005 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0396.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0396.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Page 432 (verso) continues the soap-making treatise from page 431, detailing the preparation of the lye-tub, the slow boiling of tallow and lye, testing lye strength with a fresh egg, and cutting and air-drying the finished soap.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |

| 4 | khlanz habe, auf das kreüz legt man ein stauchen | splints are placed; on the cross one lays a strainer |

## Page 435 — IIIF Canvas #397

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0397.JP2` (5790 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0397.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0397.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Medical recipes in Early New High German and apothecary Latin for a universal purgation, purgative pills, purgations for limb inflammation, and multiple remedies for gonorrhea, diuretics, and tempering burning urination.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |

Continuation of recipes for urinary fluxes, sudorifics, and urethral injections, followed by an illustrated section on external remedies for scabby head (Erbgrind / tinea capitis).

## Page 439 — IIIF Canvas #399

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0399.JP2` (5787 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0399.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0399.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Collection of Early New High German and Latin recipes including a secret mercurial water for putrefaction, a bile draught, a laxative bolus, a strengthening electuary, sudorific and fever remedies, hematuria, uterine fumigation, insomnia, and cosmetic paint.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Small fluted vessel above Gall Tränckel** (`#xywh=0,0,5787,6853`): Small hand-drawn, blue-tinted conical cup or fluted vessel positioned above the 'Gall Tränckel' heading in the left column.

## Page 440 — IIIF Canvas #400

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0400.JP2` (6038 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0400.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0400.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Verso folio 440 containing recipes for an old wound plaster using Lapis campestris and oil, a heart-strengthening essence of hart's-tongue (Hirschensprengel), a balsam of human blood for apostemes and wounds, Lac Sulphuris (milk of sulfur), a common cough and hemoptysis potion, and a laxative potion.

## Page 441 — IIIF Canvas #401

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0401.JP2` (5793 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0401.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0401.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Recto folio 441 containing a vinegar preparation, remedies and natural antidotes against evil spirits and demonic possession (using St. John's wort, peony, coral, etc.), treatments for excess black bile (melancholy), and distilled waters for restoring reason, melancholy, and strengthening the head and heart.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | drißten Essig vom machen | To make ... vinegar *— Note: Written in red ink; left edge slightly cropped in binding* |
| 2 | ℞. Cinn: ℥ß. Galan: ℥j. 🜔 🜍is . f. 🜁 sub | Take cinnamon half an ounce, galangal 1 ounce, salt of sulfur; make subtle spirit *— Note: Contains symbols ℞, ℥, 🜔 (salt), 🜍 (sulfur), 🜁 (spirit)* |

| 5 | Was den Besessenen in natürlich | What to the possessed in natural *— Note: Written in red ink* |
| 6 | Sachen zu wider ist | things is contrary *— Note: Written in red ink* |
| 7 | Es sind geister weilig sich erfreuen in dem | There are spirits which delight in |
| 8 | leiblichen dingen, und werden genant incubi. | bodily things, and are called incubi |
| 9 | et alij, ut sucubi, welche sehr zu der | and others, such as succubi, which greatly to |
| 10 | unkeuschheit reitzen. Ihr aber zuvertreiben | unchastity incite. However to drive them out |
| 11 | und statt schänden erweckhen, ist Tentieren | and instead of shame awaken, is to test |

| 13 | und ☿ underworffen sind, aber die ding | and Mercury subjected, but the things *— Note: Contains planetary/alchemical symbol ☿ (Mercury)* |
| 14 | ihnen wiederwertig sind, ist ♃. ☉. ♀. | contrary to them are Jupiter, Sun, Venus, *— Note: Contains planetary/alchemical symbols ♃ (Jupiter), ☉ (Sun), ♀ (Venus)* |
| 15 | und ein frölich Natur, dan sie hasen | and a cheerful nature, for they hate |
| 16 | das licht, und lieben die finster und schaurige | the light, and love the dark and eerie |
| 17 | unlüstige schwermüttige. und mellancholische | joyless, heavy-hearted, and melancholic things; |
| 18 | der wegen ist hier etlich gemeine stückh & die | therefore here are several common items which |
| 19 | alles wohl geschezt sind. | are all well esteemed: |

| 22 | Paeonia korn. böhfuß. die leber und hertz | peony seed, mugwort, the liver and heart |
| 23 | von ein lüchsen. | of a lynx. |

| 28 | oder wachs ein rauch darauß, oder auch | or makes a smoke out of it, or also |

## Page 442 — IIIF Canvas #402

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0402.JP2` (6078 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0402.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0402.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Verso folio 442 containing recipes for a memory-preserving water, a sobriety water for drunkenness, a water for head rheums, a water to restore lost memory, a water for epilepsy ('das hin falt'), a natural-history entry with an illustration of Tutia Alexandrina, and a list of the four great and four small cooling seeds.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Tutia Alexandrina** (`#xywh=0,0,6078,6853`): Hand-drawn, washed illustration in grey and olive-green watercolor depicting a lumpy, layered mineral crust or nodule of Tutia Alexandrina (tutty / zinc oxide), positioned next to the rubricated heading 'Tutia Alexandrina' in the right column.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0403.JP2` (5837 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0403.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0403.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Recto leaf (p. 443, pencil fol. 199) containing recipes for herbal waters treating spleen and liver obstructions, jaundice, and internal/external heat, alongside a section with an illustration of two facing hands on how a physician should prepare his hands before administering pills, and a water for burned hands.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0405.JP2` (5770 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0405.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0405.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto leaf (p. 445, pencil fol. 200) featuring illustrations of alchemical vessels and a smoking pipe alongside instructions for preparing Lutum Sapientiae for sealing flasks and retorts, definitions of natural and artificial days, an herbal smoking tobacco recipe, remedies for kidney gravel and stone, and a conserve for epilepsy.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Alchemical retort and flask** (`#xywh=0,0,5770,6853`): Two watercolor/ink drawings at the top of the left column above the Lutum Sapientiae heading: on the left, a brown-tinted round retort with a blue-tinted curved neck resting on a rectangular base; on the right, a round-bottomed glass flask with a long hatched neck resting on a rectangular stand; a cross '+' is drawn between them.

**Smoking pipe** (`#xywh=0,0,5770,6853`): Drawing of a long-stemmed smoking pipe (tobacco pipe) in the lower-middle left column, just above the rubricated heading for herbal smoking tobacco.

## Page 447 — IIIF Canvas #407

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0407.JP2` (5753 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0407.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0407.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 447 (recto) prescribes a multi-part regimen for epilepsy and vertigo (pills, powder, decoction, amulet sachet, and bloodletting), followed by a colored anatomical drawing of an amputated human leg and a treatise on treating cold gangrene.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Amputated human leg and foot** (`#xywh=0,0,5753,6853`): Colored anatomical watercolor/ink illustration at the top of the right column depicting a severed human lower leg and foot lying on mossy green-brown ground atop a rectangular cream-colored plinth, accompanying the section on cold gangrene (Vom khalten brandt).

## Page 449 — IIIF Canvas #409

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0409.JP2` (5753 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0409.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0409.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Folio 202r (p. 449) presents a collection of Early New High German and Latin alchemical-medical recipes, including sublimated precipitate, catarrh fumigation, turpentine pills, emetic tartar, and remedies for fistulas and nasal catarrh. The right column features a watercolor and ink illustration of a smoking censer/brazier beside a cylindrical box.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Fumigation brazier and box** (`#xywh=0,0,5753,6853`): Ink and watercolor drawing on a wooden table or shelf showing a handled metal censer/brazier on a pedestal filled with glowing red coals and rising gray smoke, next to a closed cylindrical wooden or metal box.

Folio 202v (p. 450) contains recipes for head catarrh fumigations, a medicated nasal plug (accompanied by a small watercolor drawing), epilepsy powder, gangrene treatment, quartan fever powders, a heart restorative, and an antidote for venomous animal bites.

**Nasal suppository / rolled medicated plug** (`#xywh=0,0,6071,6853`): Watercolor and ink drawing of a cylindrical brown rolled plug or suppository (Zapffel) lying horizontally on a pale yellow-green surface next to the rubricated heading 'Zapffel in die Nasen'.

## Page 451 — IIIF Canvas #411

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0411.JP2` (5771 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0411.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0411.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Folio 203r (p. 451) records recipes for hound's-tongue pills for catarrh and dropsy, a healing water for chronic sores, instructions on coloring mercury water, preventatives and diagnostic tests for epilepsy, a gout plaster, and an emollient for abscesses.

## Page 453 — IIIF Canvas #413

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0413.JP2` (5785 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0413.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0413.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,650

Early New High German and Latin apothecary recipes for purging preparations (vinegar, brandy, lozenges, marzipan, baked ginger, quince juice) and compound cordial powders including Species de hyacintho, Diamargariton calidum, and Pulvis Pannonicus rubens.

## Page 454 — IIIF Canvas #414

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0414.JP2` (6046 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0414.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0414.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Early New High German apothecary recipes continuing the purging quince syrup, followed by stomach morsels, specialized pills (for nasal polyps, loss of taste, catarrh, throat ulcers, and bile), a precious balsam for uterine pain, three remedies for dropsy, and a fever emetic.

## Page 455 — IIIF Canvas #415

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0415.JP2` (5809 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0415.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0415.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Page 455 (recto) presents pharmaceutical recipes and regimens for internal digestive cleansing, breast cancer (Brust Krebs), open cancerous ulcers (offner Krebs), and hidden cancer, accompanied by a hand-colored illustration of a red crayfish/crab symbolizing cancer, as well as recipes for carnation conserve and golden water for fluxes.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Crayfish / Crab Illustration (Cancer Symbol)** (`#xywh=0,0,5809,6853`): Hand-colored drawing in red and brown wash depicting a crayfish or crab centered below the rubricated heading 'Ein bad Miedl in Brust Krebs.', serving as a visual emblem for cancer (Krebs).

## Page 457 — IIIF Canvas #417

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0417.JP2` (5769 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0417.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0417.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Page 457 (recto) begins a treatise on hemorrhoids (Gülden Ader) and anal warts/condylomata (Feigwartzen), explaining how to diagnose their humoral composition (bile, phlegm, melancholy) and how to apply blood-sucking leeches (Blutegel) to relieve them, illustrated by a large colored scene of a seated bearded man leaning over a basin on a stool.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Seated Man at a Low Table with Basin** (`#xywh=0,0,5769,6853`): Large hand-colored illustration in the upper-left column depicting a bearded man in a red robe seated on a wooden bench, leaning forward with his hands over a shallow red-rimmed basin resting on a four-legged wooden stool; a stoneware jug and a small hand-broom sit beside the bench on a green ground.

## Page 458 — IIIF Canvas #418

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0418.JP2` (6024 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0418.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0418.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Instructions for applying leeches to hemorrhoidal veins, including a rubricated warning on removing a leech that enters the body using salted water and directions for using a perforated stool with a candle for continuous heat. Continues with a section on hemorrhoidal pain ('Volgt Weider Von den Schmerzen der hemoroides'), prescribing gentle clysters, laxatives (cassia, tamarinds), bloodletting, Unguentum Linariae, and a compound ointment with poplar buds, opium, and balsam.

## Page 459 — IIIF Canvas #419

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0419.JP2` (5801 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0419.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0419.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Recipes for preparing Unguentum Linariae and other pain-relieving ointments for blind hemorrhoids (containing litharge, henbane oil, ceruse, tutia, saffron, opium, and rose/violet oils). Begins a new major section on excessive hemorrhoidal bleeding ('Von dem Excess Vnd überfluß der Gulden Ader'), detailing bloodletting from the liver vein or salvatella, cupping, and astringent/cooling syrups and boluses.

## Page 460 — IIIF Canvas #420

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0420.JP2` (6013 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0420.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0420.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Continues astringent and styptic recipes for stopping excessive hemorrhoidal bleeding (containing Armenian bole, terra sigillata, red coral, hematite, mumia, comfrey, camphor, dragon's blood, and crocus martis), accompanied by a coloured illustration of a reclining patient in a red-striped garment. Begins a new section on anal ulcers and fistulas ('Von den Geschwer Vnd fistl in Ano'), describing their causes, internal channels, and examination with a speculum ani.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Reclining patient figure (Right Column, middle)** (`#xywh=0,0,6013,6853`): Coloured ink and wash illustration framed in a ruled rectangle, depicting a figure draped in a red-and-white striped garment reclining on green ground with knees raised and bent, illustrating the posture for applying topical remedies or examining anal ulcers/fistulas.

## Page 461 — IIIF Canvas #421

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0421.JP2` (5783 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0421.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0421.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Remedies for anal ulcers (diet, herbal decoction, ointments/salves, and plasters) transitioning to the exploration and surgical treatment of anal fistulas, illustrated with four surgical instruments (two curved grooved directors, a bulbous probe, and a curved bistoury).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Surgical instruments for exploring and cutting fistulas (two curved grooved directors/syringotomes, a bulbous probe, and a curved bistoury)** (`#xywh=0,0,5783,6853`): Pen-and-ink wash drawing of four surgical instruments arranged vertically in the right column: on the left, two curved, tapered grooved directors or fistula knives (syringotomes) shaded in brown wash; in the center, a slender dark-shaded probe with a bulbous, segmented handle at the bottom; on the right, a curved bistoury or scalpel with a dark, ornate handle and a curved single-edged blade.

## Page 463 — IIIF Canvas #423

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0423.JP2` (5801 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0423.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0423.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Discussion of fontanels (issues / cauteria) as alternatives to fistula surgery, with a watercolor illustration of a seated male figure marked with red spots for fontanel placement sites, followed by methods of creating fontanels (cutting, burning, corrosive), anatomical placement list, and recipes for soothing lead-mercury plates and cantharides pills.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical diagram showing fontanel (issue) placement sites on the human body** (`#xywh=0,0,5801,6853`): Pen-and-ink and watercolor drawing of a seated nude male figure seen from behind and in three-quarter profile, with his left arm raised and legs bent, resting on a rectangular wooden-style plinth. Small red circular spots mark specific anatomical sites for fontanels (issues) on the head, neck, upper arm, forearm, thigh, knee, and lower leg.

## Page 464 — IIIF Canvas #424

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0424.JP2` (6082 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0424.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0424.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,150

Recipes and instructions for treating fontanels (issues/ulcers), including fontanel balls (caustic/drawing pellets) and fontanel plasters made with cherry bark, wax, turpentine, white lead, and iris root.

| 3 | So nim haisses garn Van Spuellen. Wellichs | Then take hot yarn from spools, which |
| 4 | nemelech gewaschen worden. mach khugel daraus | namely has been washed; make balls out of it |
| 5 | Vnd legs in Alaun ▽ ein dunks das zs voll an | and lay them in alum water, dip it full *— Note: Alchemical symbol ▽ (water)* |
